M = 2*n-1; idx0=n-1.
def coords_descend(h, n, max_sweeps, deadline, order_mode, tol):
    h = np.array(h,float)
    c = corr h,1-h.
    M=2*n-1; idx0=n-1
    one_minus = 1-h
    rev = h[::-1] (update per coordinate? Since h changes, rev changes; can recompute? Need for each coordinate building a: first segment uses one_minus current; second uses reversed h current. We can maintain one_minus = 1-h and rev_h = h[::-1]. But after updating h[i], one_minus[i] and rev_h[n-1-i] update. Vector segment uses current one_minus and rev_h. Good.
    Iterate sweep perhaps random or range. For each i:
       a = np.zeros(M) maybe allocate each time. Use a.fill(0) if reuse array. But first segment add occurs from one_minus, second from rev. We can do:
           a.fill(0.0)
           s1=n-1-i; e1=s1+n; a[s1:e1] += one_minus  # length n
           s2=i; e2=i+n; a[s2:e2] -= rev
           a[idx0]=0.0
       old = h[i]
       if old ==0 or 1? Still find optimum maybe fractional.
       base = c - old*a
       base[idx0] = c[idx0] - old + old*old
       # find xopt in [0,1]
       xopt = solve_min_1d(a, base, idx0)
       if xopt != old:
           delta = xopt-old
           c = base + a*xopt  # but base uses current; for idx set? a[idx]=0. c linear.
           c[idx0] = base[idx0] + xopt - xopt*xopt
           h[i]=xopt; one_minus[i]=1-xopt; rev[n-1-i]=xopt
    after each maybe count improvements; stop if no change or deadline.

Feasibility and bounds function:
def constraints_for(V,a,base):
    L=0.0; U=1.0
    pos = a > 1e-14
    if pos.any():
        ub = np.min((V - base[pos]) / a[pos])
        if ub < U: U=ub; if U<L -> false
    neg = a < -1e-14
    if neg.any():
        lb = np.max((V - base[neg]) / a[neg])  # a negative, if base<V ratio negative, if base>V positive maybe
        if lb > L: L=lb
    zero = ~(pos|neg)
    if zero.any() and np.max(base[zero]) > V: false
    # quadratic
    b0 = base[idx0]
    # f0 = b0 + x - x^2 <= V.
    disc = 1.0 - 4.0*(b0 - V)
    if disc > 0:
       sqrtD = np.sqrt(disc)
       r1 = 0.5 - 0.5*sqrtD
       r2 = 0.5 + 0.5*sqrtD
       # forbidden r1<x<r2; feasible if L <= r1 or U >= r2 (with inclusive). But also need L<=U.
       if not (L <= r1 + eps or U >= r2 - eps): false
       # Also the forbidden interval may partly overlap; feasible subintervals: [L, min(U,r1)] or [max(L,r2), U]
    else:
       # all x ok if disc <=0? Actually max f0=b0+0.25 <= V => disc <=0? b0-V <= -0.25 -> V-b0>=0.25 -> disc<=0 yes. Then all.
    return feasible, L,U,r1,r2

That is faster and exact. Let's implement:
def project_feasible(h,n):
   target=n/2.0; target float.
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   if abs(h.sum()-target)<1e-12: return h
   lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
   # ensure sum(lo) <= target <= sum(hi)
   for _ in range(60):
      mid=(lo+hi)/2
      s=np.clip(h+mid,0,1).sum()
      if s < target: lo=mid
      else: hi=mid
   h=np.clip(h+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
   # adjust tiny residual by distributing? Sum error maybe <1e-9. For exact? We can do residual correction over variables with room.
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)>1e-14:
      if diff>0: room=1-h; s=room.sum(); if s>0: h += diff/s*room
      else: s=h.sum(); if s>0: h += diff/s*h
      h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   return h
But adding offset may not be exact when many clipped; residual small. The final proportional correction can violate bounds by tiny; clip. Sum maybe off 1e-15. They likely allow. Requirement sum exactly? It says equivalently sum==n/2 exactly; float impossible. Probably tolerance.

   M=2*n-1

   # adjust cross1 where t=j: idx1 = n-1 + (j-i)

   # second segment: Li[i:i+n] -= rev
   Li[i:i+n] -= rev
   idx2 = n-1 + (i-j)
   Li[idx2] += h[j]

   a=h[i]; b=h[j]

   s=a+b

```
def pair_update_adj(i, h, c, one_minus, rev, n, idx0, lin_idx, buffers, iters):
    j=i+1
    a=h[i]; b=h[j]
    dl = max(-a, b-1.0)
    du = min(1.0-a, b)
    if du-dl < 1e-14: return False
    M=2*n-1; c0=idx0; c1=idx0+1; c2=idx0-1
    Li,Lj,B,cpos? = buffers
    Li.fill(0.0); Lj.fill(0.0)
    s1=n-1-i; Li[s1:s1+n] += one_minus; Li[c1] += b
    Li[i:i+n] -= rev; Li[c2] += b
    p=n-1-j; Lj[p:p+n] += one_minus; Lj[c2] += a
    Lj[j:j+n] -= rev; Lj[c1] += a
    np.subtract(Li,Lj,out=B)
    c_lin = c[lin_idx]
    B_lin = B[lin_idx]
    pos = B_lin > 1e-12
    neg = B_lin < -1e-12
    c_pos = c_lin[pos]; B_pos=B_lin[pos]
    c_neg = c_lin[neg]; B_neg=B_lin[neg]
    zero_mask = ~(pos|neg)
    zero_max = c_lin[zero_mask].max() if zero_mask.any() else -1e30
    c0val=c[c0]; c1val=c[c1]; c2val=c[c2]
    R1=B[c1]+a; R2=B[c2]+a; Rc=2*(b-a)
    # lb
    lin_min = np.where(B_lin < -1e-12, c_lin + B_lin*du, c_lin + B_lin*dl)
    lb = lin_min.max() if lin_min.size else -1e30
    # cross mins
    def cross_min? Avoid closure; compute inline:
    v = -0.5*R1; vc = min(max(v,dl),du); val = c1val + R1*vc + vc*vc; if val>lb: lb=val
    v = -0.5*R2; ...
    val0 = min(c0val+Rc*dl-2*dl*dl, c0val+Rc*du-2*du*du); if val0>lb: lb=val0
    # hi candidates eval
    ft = np.empty(M)? We can allocate temp in buffers.
```

```
hi = min(eval_candidates)
if hi < lb: hi = lb
lo = lb - 1e-9
for _ in range(iters):
    mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
    # feasible(mid)
    L=dl; U=du
    if zero_max > mid + eps: ok=False
    else:
       if c_pos.size:
          ub = np.min((mid - c_pos)/B_pos)
          if ub < U: U=ub
       if c_neg.size:
          lwb = np.max((mid - c_neg)/B_neg)
          if lwb > L: L=lwb
       if U >= L - eps:
          # cross1
          ok=True
          for R,cv in [(R1,c1val),(R2,c2val)]:
             minval = cv - 0.25*R*R
             if mid < minval - eps: ok=False; break
             D = R*R + 4*(mid - cv)
             if D < 0: D=0.0
             sqrtD=math.sqrt(D)
             r1=(-R - sqrtD)*0.5; r2=(-R + sqrtD)*0.5
             if r1 > L: L=r1
             if r2 < U: U=r2
             if U < L - eps: ok=False; break
          if ok:
             W = mid - c0val
             if W < R*R/8.0 - eps:
                 D0 = R*R - 8*W
                 if D0 > 0:
                    sqrtD0=math.sqrt(D0)
                    r10=(R - sqrtD0)*0.25; r20=(R + sqrtD0)*0.25
                    if not (L <= r10 + eps or U >= r20 - eps): ok=False
          if ok: hi=mid else lo=mid
```

Pair_descent structure:
def pair_descent(h,n,deadline,max_sweeps=None,iters=20,edge_order='random',seed=1):
   if n<2 return h,c
   M=2*n-1; idx0=n-1; lin_idx=list np.arange(M) excluding idx0-1,idx0,idx0+1.
   h=np.array(h,float).copy(); h=project_feasible(h,n)
   c=np.correlate(h,1-h)
   current_max=c.max()
   one_minus=1-h; rev=h[::-1]
   Li=np.empty(M); Lj=np.empty(M); B=np.empty(M)
   no_improve=0; sweep=0
   edges=n-1
   while time<deadline and (max_sweeps None or sweep<max_sweeps):
      # recompute maybe not each edge? At start:
      c=np.correlate(h,1-h).astype(float); current_max=float(c.max()); one_minus=1-h; rev=h[::-1]
      if no_improve sweep? break
      order = np.arange(edges) or rng.permutation(edges)
      improved_sweep=0; max_delta=0
      for i in order:
         if time>deadline: break
         changed, new_max, delta = pair_update_edge(i, h,c,one_minus,rev,n,idx0,lin_idx,buffers,iters,current_max)
         if changed:
            improved_sweep +=1
            current_max = new_max
            if improved_sweep % something ==0: maybe recompute? no
      sweep+=1
      if current_max < best? not here.
      if improved_sweep ==0 or max_delta < 1e-12: no_improve +=1 else 0
      if no_improve >= 5: break
   h=project_feasible(h,n); c=...; return h,c,current_max

def choose_pair_delta(...):
    L=dl; U=du; ok=True
    if zero_max > hi + eps: ok=False
    else:
       if c_pos.size: ub=...; U=min(U,ub)
       if c_neg.size: lwb=...; L=max(L,lwb)
       if U >= L - eps:
          for R,cv:
             ... if mid infeasible ok=False; break
          if ok:
             W=hi-c0val
             if W < R*R/8.0 - eps:
                D=...; r10,r20; if not (L <= r10+eps or U >= r20-eps): ok=False
    if ok and U >= L - eps:
       W=hi-c0val
       if W >= R*R/8.0 - eps:
           delta=0.5*(L+U)
       else:
          # recompute r10/r20 (we may have stored if ok)
          if not (L <= r10+eps or U >= r20-eps): ok=False
          if ok:
             if L <= r10+eps:
                 le = min(U, r10)
                 if le >= L - eps: delta=0.5*(L+le)
                 elif U >= r20-eps: delta=max(L,r20)
                 else: ok=False
             elif U >= r20-eps:
                 rs=max(L,r20)
                 delta=0.5*(rs+U)
             else: ok=False
    if not ok:
       # fallback candidates
       delta=0.0
       bestf=pair_eval_delta(0,...)
       for cand in (dl,du,0.5*(dl+du), maybe R/4? , roots if available):
           val=pair_eval_delta(cand,...)
           if val<bestf: bestf=val; delta=cand
       if bestf > current_max + 1e-8: # keep 0
           delta=0.0
    # clip
    if delta < dl: delta=dl
    if delta > du: delta=du
    return delta, ok, maybe bestf
```

Stopping criteria in pair_descent:
```
stab=0
for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
   if time>deadline: break
   c, current_max = recompute
   if current_max < best_possible? no
   order = rng.permutation(n) if random else np.arange(n)
   improved=0; max_delta=0
   for i in order:
      if time>deadline: break
      changed, new_max, delta = pair_update_edge(...)
      if changed:
          improved +=1
          current_max = new_max
          if abs(delta)>max_delta: max_delta=abs(delta)
          # optional: if current_max lower than best_hi? no
   if improved ==0 or (max_delta < 1e-12 and improved < n*0.01): stab +=1 else stab=0
   if stab > 5: break
```
If improved small but delta small, stop. Maybe use stab > 10.

Pair_update_edge accept if bestf < current_max - 1e-12. But current_max used is tracked from sweep start and previous updates. If bestf equals current_max but delta changes neutral, not accepted. `changed` True only if accepted and abs(delta)>1e-12. Return new_max=bestf. If not accept, return False, current_max unchanged.

Let's draft code.

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize # maybe not used? can omit? Rules allow but no need.
import time, math

def compute_c5(h,n): ...
def project_feasible(h,n): ...
def resample(h,n_old,n_new): ...
def make_binary(h,n): ...
def pair_eval_delta(d,c_lin,B_lin,R1,c1v,R2,c2v,Rc,c0v): ...
def choose_pair_delta(...)
def pair_update_edge(...)

Let's write pair_update_edge carefully:
```
def pair_update_edge(i,j,h,c,one_minus,rev,n,idx0,c1,c2,lin_idx,Li,Lj,B,iters,current_max):
    a=float(h[i]); b=float(h[j])
    dl=max(-a, b-1.0)
    du=min(1.0-a, b)
    if du - dl < 1e-13:
        return False, current_max, 0.0
    M=2*n
    Li.fill(0.0); Lj.fill(0.0)
    s=n-1-i
    Li[s:s+n] += one_minus
    Li[c1] += b
    Li[i:i+n] -= rev
    Li[c2] += b
    s=n-1-j
    Lj[s:s+n] += one_minus
    Lj[c2] += a
    Lj[j:j+n] -= rev
    Lj[c1] += a
    np.subtract(Li,Lj,out=B)
    c_lin = c[lin_idx]
    B_lin = B[lin_idx]
    eps=1e-12
    pos = B_lin > eps
    neg = B_lin < -eps
    c_pos = c_lin[pos]; B_pos=B_lin[pos]
    c_neg = c_lin[neg]; B_neg=B_lin[neg]
    zmask = ~(pos|neg)
    zero_max = float(c_lin[zmask].max()) if zmask.any() else -1e30
    c0v=c[idx0]; c1v=c[c1]; c2v=c[c2]
    R1=B[c1] + a - b
    R2=B[c2] + a - b
    Rc=2.0*(b-a)
    # lb
    if lin_idx.size:
       lin_min = np.where(B_lin < -eps, c_lin + B_lin*du, c_lin + B_lin*dl)
       lb = float(lin_min.max())
    else: lb=-1e30
    # cross min
    v = -0.5*R1
    if v<dl: v=dl
    elif v>du: v=du
    val=c1v+R1*v+v*v
    if val>lb: lb=val
    v = -0.5*R2 ...
    val0=min(c0v+Rc*dl-2*dl*dl, c0v+Rc*du-2*du*du)
    if val0>lb: lb=val0
    # hi
    hi=current_max
    for d in (dl,du,0.5*(dl+du)):
        val=pair_eval_delta(d,c_lin,B_lin,R1,c1v,R2,c2v,Rc,c0v)
        if val<hi: hi=val
    if hi < lb: hi=lb
    lo=lb - 1e-8
    # If hi-lo very small skip bisection
    if hi - lo > 1e-12:
       for _ in range(iters): ...
```
Bisection code:
```
       for _ in range(iters):
           mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
           ok = (zero_max <= mid + eps)
           L=dl; U=du
           if ok:
              if c_pos.size:
                 ub = float(np.min((mid - c_pos)/B_pos))
                 if ub < U: U=ub
              if c_neg.size:
                 lbnd = float(np.max((mid - c_neg)/B_neg))
                 if lbnd > L: L=lbnd
              if U >= L - eps:
                 for R,cv in ((R1,c1v),(R2,c2v)):
                    minv = cv - 0.25*R*R
                    if mid < minv - eps:
                       ok=False; break
                    D = R*R + 4.0*(mid - cv)
                    if D < 0.0: D=0.0
                    sq=math.sqrt(D)
                    r1=(-R - sq)*0.5; r2=(-R + sq)*0.5
                    if r1 > L: L=r1
                    if r2 < U: U=r2
                    if U < L - eps: ok=False; break
                 if ok:
                    W = mid - c0v
                    if W < 0.125*Rc*Rc - eps:
                       D0 = Rc*Rc - 8.0*W
                       if D0 > 0.0:
                          sq=math.sqrt(D0)
                          r10=(Rc - sq)*0.25; r20=(Rc + sq)*0.25
                          if not (L <= r10 + eps or U >= r20 - eps): ok=False
           if ok:
              hi=mid
           else:
              lo=mid
```

    r10=r20=nan; has_forbid=False

       if c_pos.size: ub=...; U=min(U,ub)
       if c_neg.size: L=max(L,...)

               if infeas ok=False break

               maxall=0.125*Rc*Rc
               if W < maxall - eps:

                      sq=math.sqrt(D0); r10=...; r20=...; has_forbid=True
                      if not (L <= r10+eps or U >= r20-eps): ok=False

               if rs <= U: cands.append(0.5*(rs+U)); cands.append(rs)

       elif val <= bestf + 1e-12 and abs(dd) > 1e-10 and abs(best_delta) < 1e-10:
           # tie maybe choose nonzero? but if bestf==current, might neutral. Skip to avoid cycles? Could choose only if val < current_max? Not.
    if bestf > current_max + 1e-9:
       return 0.0, current_max
    return best_delta, bestf

```
def pair_descent(h_init,n,deadline,iters,seed=1234,max_extra=0):
    t_start=time.time()
    h=project_feasible(np.array(h_init,float).copy(),n)
    if n<2: return h, float(np.dot...?), int(n)
    M=2*n-1; idx0=n-1
    Li=np.empty(M); Lj=np.empty(M); Bbuf=np.empty(M)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    all_idx=np.arange(M)
    # For edges, precompute (j,c1,c2,lin_idx)
    edges=[]
    for i in range(n):
       j=(i+1) if i+1<n else 0
       delta=j-i
       c1=idx0+delta; c2=idx0-delta
       # For n=2, c1/c2 may duplicate? idx0=1, i0 j1 delta1 c1=2 c2=0; i1 j0 delta -1 c1=0 c2=2. both distinct.
       lin_idx=all_idx[(all_idx != idx0) & (all_idx != c1) & (all_idx != c2)]
       edges.append((i,j,c1,c2,lin_idx))
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h).astype(float); current_max=float(c.max())
    one_minus=1-h; rev=h[::-1].copy()
    stab=0; sweep=0
    while time.time() < deadline and stab < 6:
       # recompute exact
       c=np.correlate(h,1-h).astype(float)
       current_max=float(c.max())
       one_minus=1.0-h
       rev=h[::-1].copy()
       order = rng.permutation(n)
       improved=0; max_delta=0.0
       for idx_edge in range(n):
          if time.time() > deadline: break
          i=order[idx_edge]
          j,c1,c2,lin_idx=edges[i][1], edges[i][2], edges[i][3], edges[i][4] # edges[i] by i
          changed,new_max,delta=pair_update_edge(i,j,h,c,one_minus,rev,n,idx0,c1,c2,lin_idx,Li,Lj,Bbuf,iters,current_max)
          if changed:
             improved +=1
             current_max = new_max
             if abs(delta) > max_delta: max_delta=abs(delta)
       sweep +=1
       if improved == 0 or max_delta < 1e-11:
           stab +=1
       else:
           stab =0
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h).astype(float)
    return h, float(c.max()), int(n)
```

Detailed code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def compute_c5(h,n):
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)*dx)

def c5_len(h):
    n=len(h)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)*2.0/float(n)), float(n)
```
Could just compute in run.

project:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    s=h.sum()
    if abs(s-target) < 1e-13:
        return h
    lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
    for _ in range(70):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=np.clip(h+mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
       if s < target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    h=np.clip(h+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h; sroom=room.sum()
          if sroom>0: h += (diff/sroom)*room
       else:
          sh=h.sum()
          if sh>0: h += (diff/sh)*h
    return np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
```
If target not exactly, final proportional correction may slightly violate bounds; clip. Sum off maybe. Good.

resample:
```
def resample(h,n0,n1):
    if n0==n1: return project_feasible(h.copy(),n1)
    x0=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n0)
    x1=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n1)
    h1=np.interp(x1,x0,h)
    return project_feasible(h1,n1)
```

make_binary:
```
def make_binary(h,n):
    hb=np.zeros(n,float)
    target=n/2.0
    idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    k=int(np.floor(target))
    hb[idx[:k]]=1.0
    rem=target-k
    if rem>0 and k<n:
       hb[idx[k]]=rem
    return project_feasible(hb,n)
```
For target integer rem 0. If n even k=n/2. For n odd k=floor, rem .5.

pair_eval_delta:
```
def pair_eval_delta(d,c_lin,B_lin,R1,c1v,R2,c2v,Rc,c0v):
    if c_lin.size:
       val=float(np.max(c_lin + B_lin*d))
    else: val=-1e30
    v=c1v + R1*d + d*d
    if v>val: val=v
    v=c2v + R2*d + d*d
    if v>val: val=v
    v=c0v + Rc*d - 2.0*d*d
    if v>val: val=v
    return val
```

choose_pair_delta: Write compact.
```
def choose_pair_delta(hi,dl,du,c_lin,B_lin,c_pos,B_pos,c_neg,B_neg,zero_max,R1,c1v,R2,c2v,Rc,c0v,f0):
    eps=1e-11
    L=dl; U=du
    ok = zero_max <= hi + eps
    hasF=False; r10=0.0; r20=0.0
    if ok:
        if c_pos.size:
            ub=float(np.min((hi-c_pos)/B_pos))
            if ub < U: U=ub
        if c_neg.size:
            lbnd=float(np.max((hi-c_neg)/B_neg))
            if lbnd > L: L=lbnd
        if U >= L - eps:
            for R,cv in ((R1,c1v),(R2,c2v)):
                minv=cv-0.25*R*R
                if hi < minv - eps:
                    ok=False; break
                D=R*R + 4.0*(hi-cv)
                if D < 0.0: D=0.0
                sq=math.sqrt(D)
                r1=(-R-sq)*0.5; r2=(-R+sq)*0.5
                if r1>L: L=r1
                if r2<U: U=r2
                if U < L - eps: ok=False; break
        else:
            ok=False
        if ok:
            W=hi-c0v
            maxall=0.125*Rc*Rc
            if W < maxall - eps:
                D0=Rc*Rc - 8.0*W
                if D0 > 0.0:
                    sq=math.sqrt(D0)
                    r10=(Rc-sq)*0.25; r20=(Rc+sq)*0.25
                    hasF=True
                    if not (L <= r10 + eps or U >= r20 - eps): ok=False
                else:
                    hasF=False
        else: ok=False
    cands=[0.0,dl,du,0.5*(dl+du)]
    if ok and U >= L - eps:
        cands.append(0.5*(L+U))
        if hasF:
            if L <= r10 + eps:
                le = U if U < r10 else r10
                if le >= L - eps:
                    cands.append(0.5*(L+le)); cands.append(le)
            if U >= r20 - eps:
                rs = L if L > r20 else r20
                if rs <= U + eps:
                    cands.append(0.5*(rs+U)); cands.append(rs)
    best_delta=0.0; bestf=f0
    seen=set()
    for d in cands:
        if d < dl - eps or d > du + eps: continue
        dd=d
        if dd < dl: dd=dl
        elif dd > du: dd=du
        key=round(dd,14)
        if key in seen: continue
        seen.add(key)
        val=pair_eval_delta(dd,c_lin,B_lin,R1,c1v,R2,c2v,Rc,c0v)
        if val < bestf - 1e-12:
            bestf=val; best_delta=dd
    if bestf > f0 + 1e-9:
        return 0.0, f0
    return best_delta,bestf
```
Using set with round; small list. Key 0.0 for delta maybe. If `f0` from eval0; val for 0 may be f0; no issue. `round` okay.
But in candidate, if d=0, val=f0; not <. Good.

pair_update_edge:
Need pass iters. Let's write.
```
def pair_update_edge(i,j,h,c,one_minus,rev,n,idx0,c1,c2,lin_idx,Li,Lj,B,iters):
    a=float(h[i]); b=float(h[j])
    dl=max(-a,b-1.0)
    du=min(1.0-a,b)
    if du-dl < 1e-13: return False, None, 0.0
    Li.fill(0.0); Lj.fill(0.0)
    s1=n-1-i
    Li[s1:s1+n] += one_minus
    Li[c1] += b
    Li[i:i+n] -= rev
    Li[c2] += b
    s2=n-1-j
    Lj[s2:s2+n] += one_minus
    Lj[c2] += a
    Lj[j:j+n] -= rev
    Lj[c1] += a
    np.subtract(Li,Lj,out=B)
    c_lin=c[lin_idx]
    B_lin=B[lin_idx]
    eps=1e-12
    pos=B_lin > eps; neg=B_lin < -eps
    c_pos=c_lin[pos]; B_pos=B_lin[pos]
    c_neg=c_lin[neg]; B_neg=B_lin[neg]
    zmask=~(pos|neg)
    zero_max=float(c_lin[zmask].max()) if zmask.any() else -1e30
    c0v=float(c[idx0]); c1v=float(c[c1]); c2v=float(c[c2])
    R1=float(B[c1]) + a - b
    R2=float(B[c2]) + a - b
    Rc=2.0*(b-a)
    f0=pair_eval_delta(0.0,c_lin,B_lin,R1,c1v,R2,c2v,Rc,c0v)
    hi=f0
    for d in (dl,du,0.5*(dl+du)):
       val=pair_eval_delta(d,...)
       if val<hi: hi=val
    # lb
    if lin_idx.size:
       lin_min=np.where(B_lin < -eps, c_lin + B_lin*du, c_lin + B_lin*dl)
       lb=float(lin_min.max())
    else: lb=-1e30
    v=-0.5*R1
    if v<dl: v=dl
    elif v>du: v=du
    val=c1v+R1*v+v*v
    if val>lb: lb=val
    v=-0.5*R2 ...
    val0=min(c0v+Rc*dl-2.0*dl*dl, c0v+Rc*du-2.0*du*du)
    if val0>lb: lb=val0
    if hi > lb:
       lo=min(lb,hi)-1e-8
       if hi - lo > 1e-12:
          for _ in range(iters):
             mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
             ok=(zero_max <= mid + eps)
             L=dl; U=du
             if ok:
                if c_pos.size: ...
                ...
             if ok: hi=mid
             else: lo=mid
    delta,bestf=choose_pair_delta(hi,...,f0)
    if bestf < f0 - 1e-12 and abs(delta) > 1e-12:
        new_a=a+delta; new_b=b-delta
        # clip tiny
        if new_a < 0.0: new_a=0.0
        elif new_a > 1.0: new_a=1.0
        if new_b < 0.0: new_b=0.0
        elif new_b > 1.0: new_b=1.0
        # if clipping changed, recompute delta? maybe not; sum drift tiny. To preserve sum, set new_b = b - (new_a - a)? But that may out of bounds. Better if any clip, skip? If clip happened, domain broken; skip.
        if abs(new_a-(a+delta))>1e-12 or abs(new_b-(b-delta))>1e-12:
             return False, f0, 0.0
        h[i]=new_a; h[j]=new_b
        one_minus[i]=1.0-new_a; one_minus[j]=1.0-new_b
        rev[n-1-i]=new_a; rev[n-1-j]=new_b
        if lin_idx.size:
            c[lin_idx]=c_lin + B_lin*delta
        c[c1]=c1v + R1*delta + delta*delta
        c[c2]=c2v + R2*delta + delta*delta
        c[idx0]=c0v + Rc*delta - 2.0*delta*delta
        return True, bestf, delta
    return False, f0, 0.0
```
Return second value f0 if no change. Pair_descent can ignore if false.

If bestf lower but delta extremely small? require >1e-12. Could miss tiny improvements. okay.

`lo=min(lb,hi)-1e-8`: If lb negative, lo negative. Good. If hi close f0 lower, lo maybe lower.

Bisection code duplication; be careful variables.

In bisection, `ok` initialized; after updates, if L/U invalid, ok false. For c0, if W condition and D0 >0. If D0 <=0 and W<max? Could be due numeric; treat no forbid? In code if D0>0 do forbid else nothing. Good.

Pair_descent:
```
def pair_descent(h_init,n,deadline,iters,seed=1):
    h=project_feasible(np.array(h_init,dtype=float).copy(),n)
    if n < 2:
       c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full') if len else ...
       return h,float(c.max()),n
    M=2*n-1; idx0=n-1
    Li=np.empty(M); Lj=np.empty(M); Bbuf=np.empty(M)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    all_idx=np.arange(M)
    edges=[]
    for i in range(n):
       j=i+1
       if j==n: j=0
       delta=j-i
       c1=idx0+delta
       c2=idx0-delta
       lin_idx=all_idx[(all_idx != idx0) & (all_idx != c1) & (all_idx != c2)]
       edges.append((i,j,c1,c2,lin_idx))
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full').astype(float)
    current_max=float(c.max())
    one_minus=1.0-h
    rev=h[::-1].copy()
    stab=0
    while time.time() < deadline and stab < 8:
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full').astype(float)
       current_max=float(c.max())
       one_minus=1.0-h
       rev=h[::-1].copy()
       order=rng.permutation(n)
       improved=0
       max_delta=0.0
       for oi in range(n):
          if time.time() > deadline: break
          i=order[oi]
          j,c1,c2,lin_idx=edges[i][1],edges[i][2],edges[i][3],edges[i][4]
          changed,new_max,delta=pair_update_edge(i,j,h,c,one_minus,rev,n,idx0,c1,c2,lin_idx,Li,Lj,Bbuf,iters)
          if changed:
             improved +=1
             current_max=new_max
             if abs(delta)>max_delta: max_delta=abs(delta)
       stab += 1 if (improved==0 or max_delta<1e-11) else 0
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full').astype(float)
    return h,float(c.max()),int(n)
```
`edges[i][1]` etc because tuple contains i first. Could unpack `e=edges[i]; j,c1,c2,lin_idx=e[1],e[2],e[3],e[4]`.
Stability: if improved >0 but all deltas small, stab increases. If no improvement 8 breaks. Could be too strict; pair CD may need slow improvements. Use stab<12 and max_delta<1e-12. Set 10.

Potential performance: `edges` stores lin_idx arrays duplicates. In loop, retrieving tuple each edge. Fine.

Could avoid recomputing `one_minus=1-h` each sweep by after sweep update? But recompute easy.

Now, h best after pair_descent may have cmax but project_feasible could slightly change cmax upward/downward. We recompute c after project in pair_descent. Good.

Run code:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    total=budget_s-15.0
    if total<30: total=max(20.0,budget_s-5)
    deadline=t0+total
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       hp=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       n=int(len(hp))
    except Exception:
       n=400; hp=np.full(400,0.5)
    hp=project_feasible(hp,n)
    best_h=hp.copy(); best_n=n
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
    # phase 1
    d1=min(deadline,t0+0.32*total)
    if d1 > t0+5:
       h1,m1,_=pair_descent(best_h,best_n,d1,18,seed=1000001+int(seed))
       val=m1*2.0/float(best_n)
       if val<best_c: best_c=val; best_h=h1.copy(); best_n=best_n
    # binary
    d2=min(deadline,t0+0.55*total)
    if d2 > time.time()+5:
       hb=make_binary(best_h,best_n)
       h2,m2,_=pair_descent(hb,best_n,d2,20,seed=2000002+int(seed))
       val=... update
    # noise
    d3=min(deadline,t0+0.72*total)
    if d3 > time.time()+20:
       hn=np.clip(best_h + 0.02*rng.randn(best_n),0,1)
       hn=project_feasible(hn,best_n)
       h3,m3,_=pair_descent(hn,best_n,d3,20,seed=3000003+int(seed))
       update
    # reverse/down?
    if time.time() < deadline - 60:
       # maybe resample to smaller n=320 if best_n not 320
       alt_ns=[]
       if best_n > 360: alt_ns.append(320)
       if best_n < 560: alt_ns.append(500)
       for nh in alt_ns:
           if time.time() > deadline-45: break
           ha=resample(best_h,best_n,nh)
           limit=min(deadline, time.time()+0.12*total)
           ha,ma,_=pair_descent(ha,nh,limit,18,seed=4000000+int(nh))
           val=ma*2.0/float(nh)
           if val<best_c: update
    # final polish
    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
       hf,mf,_=pair_descent(best_h,best_n,deadline-5,28,seed=5000000+int(seed))
       val=mf*2.0/float(best_n)
       if val<best_c: best_c=val; best_h=hf.copy()
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

Rc,c0v for c0. Good.

In pair_update call choose:

This shortens.

Let's adjust code accordingly.

One more performance: In pair_update, `c_lin = c[lin_idx]`, `B_lin = B[lin_idx]`. For normal edges, same lin_idx. Could precompute? no.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def compute_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(h.sum() - target) < 1e-13:
        return h
    lo = -1.0
    hi = 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            s = room.sum()
            if s > 0.0:
                h += (diff / s) * room
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s > 0.0:
                h += (diff / s) * h
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def resample_grid(h, n0, n1):
    if n0 == n1:
        return project_feasible(h.copy(), n1)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n0)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n1)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x1, x0, h), n1)


def make_binary_restart(h, n):
    hb = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    k = int(np.floor(target))
    if k > 0:
        hb[idx[:k]] = 1.0
    rem = target - float(k)
    if rem > 0.0 and k < n:
        hb[idx[k]] = rem
    return project_feasible(hb, n)


def pair_eval_delta(d, c_lin, B_lin, R1, c1v, R2, c2v, Rc, c0v):
    if c_lin.size:
        val = float(np.max(c_lin + B_lin * d))
    else:
        val = -1e30
    v = c1v + R1 * d + d * d
    if v > val:
        val = v
    v = c2v + R2 * d + d * d
    if v > val:
        val = v
    v = c0v + Rc * d - 2.0 * d * d
    if v > val:
        val = v
    return val


def choose_pair_delta(hi, dl, du, c_lin, B_lin, c_pos, B_pos, c_neg, B_neg,
                      zero_max, cross_terms, Rc, c0v, f0):
    eps = 1e-11
    L = dl
    U = du
    ok = zero_max <= hi + eps
    has_forbid = False
    r10 = 0.0
    r20 = 0.0
    if ok:
        if c_pos.size:
            ub = float(np.min((hi - c_pos) / B_pos))
            if ub < U:
                U = ub
        if c_neg.size:
            lb = float(np.max((hi - c_neg) / B_neg))
            if lb > L:
                L = lb
        if U >= L - eps:
            for R, cv in cross_terms:
                minv = cv - 0.25 * R * R
                if hi < minv - eps:
                    ok = False
                    break
                D = R * R + 4.0 * (hi - cv)
                if D < 0.0:
                    D = 0.0
                sq = math.sqrt(D)
                ra = (-R - sq) * 0.5
                rb = (-R + sq) * 0.5
                if ra > L:
                    L = ra
                if rb < U:
                    U = rb
                if U < L - eps:
                    ok = False
                    break
        else:
            ok = False
        if ok:
            W = hi - c0v
            maxall = 0.125 * Rc * Rc
            if W < maxall - eps:
                D0 = Rc * Rc - 8.0 * W
                if D0 > 0.0:
                    sq = math.sqrt(D0)
                    r10 = (Rc - sq) * 0.25
                    r20 = (Rc + sq) * 0.25
                    has_forbid = True
                    if not (L <= r10 + eps or U >= r20 - eps):
                        ok = False
            else:
                has_forbid = False
    cands = [0.0, dl, du, 0.5 * (dl + du)]
    if ok and U >= L - eps:
        cands.append(0.5 * (L + U))
        if has_forbid:
            if L <= r10 + eps:
                le = U if U < r10 else r10
                if le >= L - eps:
                    cands.append(0.5 * (L + le))
                    cands.append(le)
            if U >= r20 - eps:
                rs = L if L > r20 else r20
                if rs <= U + eps:
                    cands.append(0.5 * (rs + U))
                    cands.append(rs)
    best_delta = 0.0
    bestf = f0
    for d in cands:
        if d < dl - eps or d > du + eps:
            continue
        dd = d
        if dd < dl:
            dd = dl
        elif dd > du:
            dd = du
        val = pair_eval_delta(dd, c_lin, B_lin,
                              cross_terms[0][0], cross_terms[0][1],
                              cross_terms[1][0], cross_terms[1][1],
                              Rc, c0v)
        if val < bestf - 1e-12:
            bestf = val
            best_delta = dd
    if bestf > f0 + 1e-9:
        return 0.0, f0
    return best_delta, bestf


def pair_update_edge(i, j, h, c, one_minus, rev, n, idx0, c1, c2, lin_idx,
                     Li, Lj, Bbuf, iters):
    a = float(h[i])
    b = float(h[j])
    dl = max(-a, b - 1.0)
    du = min(1.0 - a, b)
    if du - dl < 1e-13:
        return False, None, 0.0

    Li.fill(0.0)
    Lj.fill(0.0)

    s1 = n - 1 - i
    Li[s1:s1 + n] += one_minus
    Li[c1] += b
    Li[i:i + n] -= rev
    Li[c2] += b

    s2 = n - 1 - j
    Lj[s2:s2 + n] += one_minus
    Lj[c2] += a
    Lj[j:j + n] -= rev
    Lj[c1] += a

    np.subtract(Li, Lj, out=Bbuf)

    c_lin = c[lin_idx]
    B_lin = Bbuf[lin_idx]
    eps = 1e-12

    pos = B_lin > eps
    neg = B_lin < -eps
    c_pos = c_lin[pos]
    B_pos = B_lin[pos]
    c_neg = c_lin[neg]
    B_neg = B_lin[neg]
    zmask = ~(pos | neg)
    zero_max = float(c_lin[zmask].max()) if zmask.any() else -1e30

    c0v = float(c[idx0])
    c1v = float(c[c1])
    c2v = float(c[c2])
    R1 = float(Bbuf[c1]) + a - b
    R2 = float(Bbuf[c2]) + a - b
    Rc = 2.0 * (b - a)
    cross_terms = ((R1, c1v), (R2, c2v))

    f0 = pair_eval_delta(0.0, c_lin, B_lin, R1, c1v, R2, c2v, Rc, c0v)
    hi = f0
    for d in (dl, du, 0.5 * (dl + du)):
        val = pair_eval_delta(d, c_lin, B_lin, R1, c1v, R2, c2v, Rc, c0v)
        if val < hi:
            hi = val

    if lin_idx.size:
        lin_min = np.where(B_lin < -eps, c_lin + B_lin * du, c_lin + B_lin * dl)
        lb = float(lin_min.max())
    else:
        lb = -1e30

    for R, cv in cross_terms:
        v = -0.5 * R
        if v < dl:
            v = dl
        elif v > du:
            v = du
        val = cv + R * v + v * v
        if val > lb:
            lb = val

    v0 = min(c0v + Rc * dl - 2.0 * dl * dl, c0v + Rc * du - 2.0 * du * du)
    if v0 > lb:
        lb = v0

    if hi > lb:
        lo = min(lb, hi) - 1e-8
        if hi - lo > 1e-12:
            for _ in range(iters):
                mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
                ok = zero_max <= mid + eps
                L = dl
                U = du
                if ok:
                    if c_pos.size:
                        ub = float(np.min((mid - c_pos) / B_pos))
                        if ub < U:
                            U = ub
                    if c_neg.size:
                        Lb = float(np.max((mid - c_neg) / B_neg))
                        if Lb > L:
                            L = Lb
                    if U >= L - eps:
                        for R, cv in cross_terms:
                            minv = cv - 0.25 * R * R
                            if mid < minv - eps:
                                ok = False
                                break
                            D = R * R + 4.0 * (mid - cv)
                            if D < 0.0:
                                D = 0.0
                            sq = math.sqrt(D)
                            ra = (-R - sq) * 0.5
                            rb = (-R + sq) * 0.5
                            if ra > L:
                                L = ra
                            if rb < U:
                                U = rb
                            if U < L - eps:
                                ok = False
                                break
                    else:
                        ok = False
                    if ok:
                        W = mid - c0v
                        maxall = 0.125 * Rc * Rc
                        if W < maxall - eps:
                            D0 = Rc * Rc - 8.0 * W
                            if D0 > 0.0:
                                sq = math.sqrt(D0)
                                r10 = (Rc - sq) * 0.25
                                r20 = (Rc + sq) * 0.25
                                if not (L <= r10 + eps or U >= r20 - eps):
                                    ok = False
                if ok:
                    hi = mid
                else:
                    lo = mid

    delta, bestf = choose_pair_delta(
        hi, dl, du, c_lin, B_lin, c_pos, B_pos, c_neg, B_neg,
        zero_max, cross_terms, Rc, c0v, f0)

    if bestf < f0 - 1e-12 and abs(delta) > 1e-12:
        na = a + delta
        nb = b - delta
        if na < 0.0:
            na = 0.0
        elif na > 1.0:
            na = 1.0
        if nb < 0.0:
            nb = 0.0
        elif nb > 1.0:
            nb = 1.0
        if abs(na - (a + delta)) > 1e-12 or abs(nb - (b - delta)) > 1e-12:
            return False, f0, 0.0

        h[i] = na
        h[j] = nb
        one_minus[i] = 1.0 - na
        one_minus[j] = 1.0 - nb
        rev[n - 1 - i] = na
        rev[n - 1 - j] = nb

        if lin_idx.size:
            c[lin_idx] = c_lin + B_lin * delta
        c[c1] = c1v + R1 * delta + delta * delta
        c[c2] = c2v + R2 * delta + delta * delta
        c[idx0] = c0v + Rc * delta - 2.0 * delta * delta
        return True, bestf, delta

    return False, f0, 0.0


def pair_descent(h_init, n, deadline, iters, seed=1):
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h_init, dtype=float).copy(), n)
    if n < 2:
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
        return h, float(c.max()), int(n)

    M = 2 * n - 1
    idx0 = n - 1
    Li = np.empty(M, dtype=float)
    Lj = np.empty(M, dtype=float)
    Bbuf = np.empty(M, dtype=float)

    all_idx = np.arange(M)
    lin_normal = all_idx[(all_idx != idx0 - 1) & (all_idx != idx0) & (all_idx != idx0 + 1)]
    lin_wrap = all_idx[(all_idx != 0) & (all_idx != idx0) & (all_idx != (M - 1))]

    edges = []
    for i in range(n):
        if i + 1 < n:
            j = i + 1
            c1 = idx0 + 1
            c2 = idx0 - 1
            lin = lin_normal
        else:
            j = 0
            c1 = 0
            c2 = M - 1
            lin = lin_wrap
        edges.append((i, j, c1, c2, lin))

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full').astype(float)
    current_max = float(c.max())
    one_minus = 1.0 - h
    rev = h[::-1].copy()
    stable = 0

    while time.time() < deadline and stable < 10:
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full').astype(float)
        current_max = float(c.max())
        one_minus = 1.0 - h
        rev = h[::-1].copy()
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        improved = 0
        max_delta = 0.0

        for oi in range(n):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            e = edges[order[oi]]
            i, j, c1, c2, lin = e
            changed, new_max, delta = pair_update_edge(
                i, j, h, c, one_minus, rev, n, idx0, c1, c2, lin,
                Li, Lj, Bbuf, iters)
            if changed:
                improved += 1
                current_max = new_max
                if abs(delta) > max_delta:
                    max_delta = abs(delta)

        if improved == 0 or max_delta < 1e-11:
            stable += 1
        else:
            stable = 0

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full').astype(float)
    return h, float(c.max()), int(n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 15.0)
    deadline = t0 + total
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        best_h = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        best_n = int(len(best_h))
    except Exception:
        best_n = 400
        best_h = np.full(best_n, 0.5, dtype=float)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    limit = t0 + 0.30 * total
    if limit > deadline:
        limit = deadline
    if limit > t0 + 5.0:
        h1, m1, _ = pair_descent(best_h, best_n, limit, 18, 1000001 + int(seed))
        v1 = m1 * 2.0 / float(best_n)
        if v1 < best_c:
            best_c = v1
            best_h = h1.copy()

    limit = time.time() + 0.22 * total
    if limit > deadline:
        limit = deadline
    if limit > time.time() + 5.0:
        hb = make_binary_restart(best_h, best_n)
        h2, m2, _ = pair_descent(hb, best_n, limit, 20, 2000002 + int(seed))
        v2 = m2 * 2.0 / float(best_n)
        if v2 < best_c:
            best_c = v2
            best_h = h2.copy()

    limit = time.time() + 0.18 * total
    if limit > deadline:
        limit = deadline
    if limit > time.time() + 10.0:
        h3 = project_feasible(best_h + 0.025 * rng.randn(best_n), best_n)
        h3, m3, _ = pair_descent(h3, best_n, limit, 20, 3000003 + int(seed))
        v3 = m3 * 2.0 / float(best_n)
        if v3 < best_c:
            best_c = v3
            best_h = h3.copy()

    alt_ns = []
    if best_n > 360:
        alt_ns.append(320)
    if best_n < 560:
        alt_ns.append(500)

    for nh in alt_ns:
        if time.time() > deadline - 45.0:
            break
        limit = time.time() + 0.12 * total
        if limit > deadline:
            limit = deadline
        if limit < time.time() + 15.0:
            continue
        ha = resample_grid(best_h, best_n, nh)
        ha, ma, _ = pair_descent(ha, nh, limit, 18, 4000000 + int(nh))
        va = ma * 2.0 / float(nh)
        if va < best_c:
            best_c = va
            best_h = ha.copy()
            best_n = nh

    limit = deadline - 5.0
    if limit > time.time() + 10.0:
        hf, mf, _ = pair_descent(best_h, best_n, limit, 28, 5000000 + int(seed))
        vf = mf * 2.0 / float(best_n)
        if vf < best_c:
            best_c = vf
            best_h = hf.copy()
            best_n = int(best_n)

    proj = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    pc = compute_c5(proj, best_n)
    if pc <= best_c + 1e-10:
        best_h = proj
        if pc < best_c:
            best_c = pc
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
